CONSTANTS:
    Q      = 10201          // number of neurons (training samples)
    R      = 2          // input dimension
    spread = 2.000109035       // spread of the radial basis functions
    b      = 0.8326/spread = 0.4162773056
    // equivalent Gaussian sigma (Nadaraya-Watson form): 1.698750667

ALGORITHM Evaluate_GRNN(x[1..R]) RETURNS y:
    num <- 0
    den <- 0
    FOR i <- 1 TO Q DO
        // Euclidean distance to the centre of neuron i
        d <- sqrt( sum_{j=1..R} ( x[j] - c[i][j] )^2 )
        // Radial basis response of neuron i
        a <- exp( -( b * d )^2 )
        num <- num + w[i] * a
        den <- den + a
    END FOR
    y <- num / den
END ALGORITHM
